242 Guru Teak Investments ( Mysore) P Ltd Provisional registration granted by SEBI to the CIS entity expired. Vide Order dated February 18, 2005, the CIS entity was directed by SEBI to wind up its schemes and repay the investors in accordance with the SEBI CIS Regulations within a period of 5 and 1/2 months from the date of receipt of the Order. The CIS entity filed an appeal in Securities Appellate Tribunal against the said Order of SEBI.
